# Mosquito Shortbow
Aliases: Mosquito Bow
Categories: Legendary items, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Weapons, Craftable items, Bows, Slayer, Spider Slayer
[Mosquito Shortbow]
Type: Bow ([[Bows]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Spider Slayer VII (7) ([[Spider Slayer]])

Shot Cooldown: 0.5s

Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 1 [[Digested Mosquito]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MOSQUITO_BOW
Tooltip: Damage: +319; Strength: +151; Crit Damage: +39%; Vitality: +20; Shot Cooldown: 0.5s; Ability: Eggsecute Arrows fired from this weapon deal 3x damage to Egg Sacs spawned by the Tarantula Broodfather!; Ability: Nasty Bite LEFT CLICK Shoot an enhanced shot, healing you for 189 Health on hit.; Vitality Cost: 10; Shortbow: Instantly shoots!; This item can be reforged!; ☠ Requires Spider Slayer 7.; LEGENDARY BOW
The Mosquito Shortbow is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Bows|Bow]] unlocked at [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer VII (7)]]. Its ability shoots a more powerful arrow healing 189 [[Health]] at a 10 [[Vitality]] cost. It is the last bow unlocked in the [[Spider Slayer]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Spider Slayer VII (7) ([[Spider Slayer]])|1x [[Digested Mosquito]] 1x [[Spider Shortbow]] 2x [[Tarantula Catalyst]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Tarantula Silk]]|Recipe: [[Tarantula Catalyst]] ×2 + [[Tarantula Silk]] x64 ×2 + [[Digested Mosquito]] + [[Spider Shortbow]] → Mosquito Shortbow|
## Usage
Bow:
Mosquito Shortbow [[Damage]]: +319 [[Strength]]: +151 [[Crit Damage]]: +39 [[Vitality]]: +20
[Effects]
[Ability: Eggsecute; Arrows fired from this weapon deal 3x damage to Egg Sacs spawned by the Tarantula Broodfather Tarantula Broodfather! Ability: Nasty Bite LEFT CLICK; Shoot an enhanced shot, healing you for 189 Health on hit.; Vitality Cost (Vitality): 10; Cooldown: 0.5s]
The Mosquito Shortbow's Nasty Bite can be triggered upon left-clicking. This shot costs 10 [[Vitality]] to fire. Every mob hit heals the player for 189 [[Health]]. This has a fixed cooldown of 0.5 seconds regardless of the player's [[Attack Speed]].
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Spider Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|30|30|
|✪|15|45|
|✪✪|25|70|
|✪✪✪|35|105|
|✪✪✪✪|45; 10,000 [[Coins]]|150; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|65; 25,000 [[Coins]]|215; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- Prior to Update 0.23.4, the Mosquito Shortbow's Nasty Bite ability had no cooldown, unlike its regular attack. As a result, players could abuse this to achieve high levels of damage by spamming the ability. It was later given a hidden cooldown of 0.5s.

# Mummy Jerry Skin
Categories: Legendary items, Pet Skins, Fire Sales
[Mummy Jerry Skin]
Type: Pet Skin ([[Pet Skins]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_SKIN_JERRY_MUMMY
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Jerry pets.; This skin adapts to the day-night cycle!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; LEGENDARY COSMETIC
The Mummy Jerry Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Mummy Jerry Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on November 2, 2024. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 7,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Mummy Jerry Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Jerry Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.
- This skin adapts to the day-night cycle!

- Night

Item ID: MUSHROOM_BARN_SKIN
Tooltip: Consume this item to unlock the Mushroom Barn Skin on The Garden!; Click to consume!; LEGENDARY COSMETIC
The Mushroom Barn Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Barn Skins|Barn Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Mushroom Barn Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on August 13, 2023. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 2,000 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 8,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Mushroom Barn Skin can be consumed on the [[The Garden|Garden]] and then used at [[The Garden|The Desk]] to change the look of the center plot.

# Mythological Dye
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Mythological Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_MYTHOLOGICAL
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #6F6F0C!; LEGENDARY DYE
Mythological Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Mythological Dye can be obtained by slaying [[Mythological|Mythological Creatures]].
|Mobs|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Minos Hunter]], Siamese Lynx ([[Siamese Lynxes]]),; [[Stranded Nymph]], [[Cretan Bull]]|0.0001% (1 in 1,000,000)|
|[[Harpy]], [[Gaia Construct]]|0.0002% (1 in 500,000)|
|[[Minotaur]], [[Minos Champion]]|0.0004% (1 in 250,000)|
|[[Sphinx]], [[Minos Inquisitor]]|0.002% (1 in 50,000)|
|[[Manticore]], [[King Minos]]|0.01% (1 in 10,000)|
## Usage
Mythological Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #6F6F0C. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

- Mythological Bucket of Dye Mythological [[Bucket of Dye]]

[Effects]
[Tiered Bonus: Unearthed; Reduces damage taken from Mythological mobs by 10% (2 Pieces)➜12.5% (3 Pieces)➜15% (4 Pieces)➜17.5% (5 Pieces)➜20% (6 Pieces)➜22.5% (7 Pieces)➜25% (8 Pieces).; Pieces Required: 2+ Tiered Bonus: Familiarity; Grants +10➜15➜20 Magic Find on Mythological mobs.; Pieces Required: 2+ Armor Tiered Bonus: Mythological Greed; Grants +10%➜15%➜20% more Coins from killing Mythological mobs.; Pieces Required: 2+ Equipment]
Mythos Armor grants 2x stats on the [[Hub Island]] while the [[Mythological Ritual]] is active. Additionally, the Unearthed, Familiarity and Mythological Greed tiered bonuses reduce damage taken and increases [[Magic Find]] against [[Mythological]] mobs and grants more [[Coins]] respectively.

# Nadeshiko Dye
Aliases: Nadeshiko Dye (Old)
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Nadeshiko Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_NADESHIKO
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #F6ADC6!; LEGENDARY DYE
Nadeshiko Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The player has a variable chance of obtaining a Nadeshiko Dye by completing Superpairs in the [[Experimentation Table]]
- 0.0013% (1 in 75,000) chance from Supreme Experiments.
- 0.002% (1 in 50,000) chance from Transcendent Experiments.
- 0.004% (1 in 25,000) chance from Metaphysical Experiments.
## Usage
Nadeshiko Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece, excluding [[Armor/Helmets|skulls]], by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #F6ADC6.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
## Gallery
- Nadeshiko Dyed Leather Armor
- Nadeshiko Bucket of Dye Nadeshiko [[Bucket of Dye]]
## History

The Naga Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Charmed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Naga Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Sea Serpent Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher and Water Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Naga Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Charmed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Charmed ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.05–0.5 [[Charm Chance]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Naga Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

The Necromancer Sword is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
A Necromancer Sword can be claimed once as a reward from [[Sadan|Sadan VI (6)]], which is unlocked after defeating [[Sadan]] 750 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
A Necromancer Sword can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|

|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]])|Bedrock|10,000,000 [[Coins]]|1.2471% (1 in 80.2 runs)|1.4134% (1 in 70.8 runs)|285|20|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|10,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.4386% (1 in 228 runs)|0.8612% (1 in 116.1 runs)|285|2|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|10,000,000 [[Coins]]|1.3248% (1 in 75.5 runs)|1.736% (1 in 57.6 runs)|285|20|
## Usage
Sword:
Necromancer Sword [[Defense]]: +125 [[Damage]]: +250 [[Strength]]: +125
[Effects]
[Ability: Raise Souls RIGHT CLICK; Monsters you kill using this item will drop their soul. You can click on their souls on the ground using this item to absorb them and then spawn them to fight by your side.; Mana cost is based on the power of the monsters you summon. Shift right-click to view and remove souls from this item. If your summoned monster dies the soul will be removed.; Cooldown: 1s; Max Souls (Necromancy): 2]
The Necromancer Sword, being a sword, can be used as a melee weapon. Similar to the [[Reaper Scythe]] and [[Summoning Ring]], the Necromancer Sword uses the [[Necromancy]] mechanic: its Raise Souls ability allows you to collect the souls of defeated mobs and summon them in combat.

## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|

## Trivia
- The wither spawned from the Witherborn full set bonus can be teleported to by using a [[Shadow Fury]] or a [[Silent Death]].
- The [[Necron Dye]] can be obtained from [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Master Mode Floor VII]], which can change the color of Leather Armor to #E7413C, which is the color of Necron's Chestplate.
- Donating Necron's Armor to the Museum grants credit for all other Wither Armor variants.
- You can disable the Witherborn full set bonus by clicking on the key bind assigned to your "Pick Block" button in the key binds settings.

|[[Scylla]]|None|8 [[Bigfoot's Bola]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] Necron's Blade (Unrefined) [[Hyperion]] [[Astraea]] [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: [[Bigfoot's Bola]] ×8 + Necron's Blade (Unrefined)/[[Hyperion]]/[[Astraea]]/[[Valkyrie]] → [[Scylla]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|150|150|
|✪✪|300|450|
|✪✪✪|500|950|
|✪✪✪✪|900; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,850; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|1,500; 25,000 [[Coins]]|3,350; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
All 4 variants have the same essence crafting costs.
## Necron's Blade Scrolls
[[Necron's Blade Scrolls]] are items that can be applied onto a Necron's Blade to give it abilities on right click. There are 3 scrolls: [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Implosion]], [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Wither Shield]], and [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Shadow Warp]]. Multiple scrolls can be applied to the same blade. Applying all 3 to the same blade combines the scrolls into a different ability, [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Wither Impact]].
## Trivia
- The 4 refined swords all have Norse or Greek names from mythology.
- Donating any of the 4 refined swords to the Museum gives credit for Necron's Blade (Unrefined), but donating a Necron's Blade (Unrefined) does not give credit for any of the Refined swords.
  - The best way to get credit for all 5 items with the minimum amount of donations is to donate a [[Hyperion]].

Categories: Legendary items, Items, Catacombs Loot, Crafting ingredients, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Necron's Handle]
Type: Dungeon Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No

Necron's Handle is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item used to craft [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]. It can be obtained from [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] (7).
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Bedrock|100,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.1067% (1 in 937 runs)|0.1176% (1 in 850.2 runs)|360|15|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|100,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.1296% (1 in 771.8 runs)|0.2003% (1 in 499.4 runs)|380|18|
Necron's Handle obtained in Master Mode has a 5% chance to be shiny, which makes weapons crafted with it display additional cosmetic effects when held.
### Raffle of the Century
Necron's Handle can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]|None|[[Necron's Handle]] 1x Necron's Handle 24x [[Wither Catalyst]]|Recipe: [[Wither Catalyst]] x12 ×2 + Necron's Handle → [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]|

The Neon Blue Ender Dragon Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Neon Blue Ender Dragon Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 8, 2021. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 2,500 purchases.

The Neon Blue Ender Dragon Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Ender Dragon Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

The Nessie Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Loch Hook]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Nessie Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Nessie]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
A Nessie Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Nessie Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Loch Hook]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Loch Hook ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Double Hook Chance]] while on Foraging Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Nessie Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Nessie Shard has the following description:
- Sightings of Nessie are occasionally reported by residents of the Moonglade Marsh, but she is believed by most to be nothing but a myth.

[Item Properties]

Item ID: CORRUPTED_NETHER_STAR
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; LEGENDARY
Nether Stars are [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] items that are dropped from [[Vanquisher|Vanquishers]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Nether Star are [[Drop Chance|1Guaranteed]] drops by [[Vanquisher]], which has a chance to spawn when any mob is killed on [[Crimson Isle]]. The player that originally spawned it gets 3, every player who contributed to killing it gets one and the player who lands the killing blow gets an additional Nether Star.
### Fishing
Nether Star can be caught while [[Treasure|Treasure Fishing]] in [[Crimson Isle]]:
|Item|Catch Type|Weight|Pool|
|-|-|-|-|
|4 Nether Star|OUTSTANDING CATCH ([[Treasure]])|25|12.32%|
|1 Nether Star|GREAT CATCH ([[Treasure]])|20|1.36%|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Vanquished Ghast Cloak]]|Ghast Tear VII (7) ([[Ghast Tear]])|1x [[Ghast Cloak]] [[Nether Star]] 3x Nether Star 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Silver Fang]]|Recipe: Nether Star ×3 + [[Silver Fang]] x64 ×4 + [[Ghast Cloak]] → [[Vanquished Ghast Cloak]]|
|[[Vanquished Blaze Belt]]|Blaze Rod VII (7) ([[Blaze Rod]])|1x [[Blaze Belt]] 2x [[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] [[Nether Star]] 3x Nether Star|Recipe: [[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] ×2 + [[Blaze Belt]] + Nether Star ×3 → [[Vanquished Blaze Belt]]|
|[[Vanquished Magma Necklace]]|Magma Cream VII (7) ([[Magma Cream]])|1x [[Magma Necklace]] [[Nether Star]] 3x Nether Star 256x [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] x64 ×4 + [[Magma Necklace]] + Nether Star x3 → [[Vanquished Magma Necklace]]|
|[[Vanquished Glowstone Gauntlet]]|Glowstone Dust VII (7) ([[Glowstone Dust]])|1x [[Glowstone Gauntlet]] [[Nether Star]] 3x Nether Star 4x [[Enchanted Glowstone]]|Recipe: Nether Star ×3 + [[Enchanted Glowstone]] ×4 + [[Glowstone Gauntlet]] → [[Vanquished Glowstone Gauntlet]]|
### Kuudra Keys
Nether Stars may also be used as a currency with [[Mage Emissary]] and [[Barbarian Emissary]] to purchase [[Kuudra Keys]] of any tier at the price of [[Nether Star]] 2 Nether Stars, some [[Coins]] and [[Enchanted Mycelium]] or [[Enchanted Red Sand]], depending on player's [[Factions|Faction]].

Stacks up to 5 times.; Each piece grants a 7.5% bonus gift chance for every present you earn from the Gift Attack minigame!; All stats of this armor piece are doubled while on Jerry's Workshop!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 28.; LEGENDARY HELMET
Nutcracker Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set unlocked at [[Fishing|Fishing XXVIII (28)]]. Each piece grants a 7.5% bonus gift chance on every present earned from the [[Defend Jerry's Workshop]] event during the [[Season of Jerry]], adding up to a 30% bonus when wearing the full set.

# Nyanza Dye

Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics

Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])

Item ID: DYE_NYANZA
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #E9FFDB!; LEGENDARY DYE
Nyanza Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Nyanza Dye has a 0.0004% (1 in 250,000) chance of appearing as a reward from [[Commissions]].
## Usage
Nyanza Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece, excluding [[Armor/Helmets|skulls]], by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #E9FFDB.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
## Gallery
- Nyanza Dyed Leather Armor
- Nyanza Bucket of Dye Nyanza [[Bucket of Dye]]
## Trivia
- The Nyanza Dye is rolled when a commission is handed out and is listed as a reward in advance.

Tooltip: Health: +90; Defense: +110; Gemstones: [☤]; Full Set Bonus: Old Blood (0\ 4); Increases the strength of Growth, Protection, Feather Falling, Sugar Rush, and True Protection while worn.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 16.; LEGENDARY HELMET
Old Dragon Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set that is focused on [[Health]] and increasing the effect of [[Defense]]-oriented [[Enchantments]]. The full set can be crafted from 240 (3 stacks plus 48) [[Dragon Fragment|Old Dragon Fragments]], and Old Dragon Armor is one of the 8 different [[Dragon Armor]] sets.
At least [[Combat|Combat XVI (16)]] is required to be able to use this set and, if a dungeon item, [[Catacombs|Catacombs X (10)]] to be able to use this set while in [[Dungeons]].
## Enchantment Buffs
|Enchantment|Buff|
|-|-|
|[[Growth]]|+10 [[Health]] Per Level|
|[[Protection]]|+2 [[Defense]] Per Level|
|[[Feather Falling]]|+3% Fall damage reduction Per Level|
|[[Sugar Rush]]|+1 [[Speed]] Per Level|
|[[True Protection]]|+3 [[True Defense]] Per Level|

## Trivia
- The Full Set Bonus gives buffs to [[Growth]], [[Protection]], [[Feather Falling]], [[Sugar Rush]], and [[True Protection]] on the Armor.
- Old Dragon Armor is sometimes referred to as "Boomer Drag Armor".
- If a player wears the full Old Dragon Armor set while enchanting other armors, the listed stat of the enchantments will be that of the Dragon Armor. This does not grant them the same increased enchant however.

# Overclocker 3000
Aliases: Overclock, Overclocker
Categories: Legendary items, Items
[Overclocker 3000]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 250,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: OVERCLOCKER_3000
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Permanently increases the maximum level of a Farming Tool by +1.; Can be applied up to 10 times.; Requires tool level 40+ to apply.; LEGENDARY
An Overclocker 3000 is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
An Overclocker 3000 can be obtained across various activities in [[The Garden]]:
- Trading with [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]].
- Rewarded from [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]].
- Dropped from [[Crops (disambiguation)|Crops]].
- 0.05% (1 in 2,000) chance to be dropped from the [[Greenhouse]].
- Dropped from [[Pests]].
Each activity increases the chance for an Overclocker 3000 to drop from it through the [[Pity and RNG Meters|Pity]] system up to 100%.
Upon obtaining an Overclocker 3000, the following message displays:
ABOUT TIME! You find an Overclocker 3000!
## Usage
An Overclocker 3000 can be applied to any [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] up to 10 times to increase its level cap.

## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Snow Owl Skin]]|[[Snow Owl Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Winter 2023 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Tips
- For maximum efficiency, you need three Owl Pets for pet training at [[Fann]], and one for the passive [[Taming Wisdom]] if you don't have the XP for [[Taming|Taming LX (60)]] yet (so that you gain the [[Taming]] XP buff even while all your training slots are in use).
## Trivia
- The Owl Pet, along with the [[Grandma Wolf Pet]], [[Spirit Pet]], [[Parrot Pet]], [[Kuudra Pet]] and [[Bingo Pet]] are the only pets in the game who grants perks even when unequipped.
- The Owl Pet is the first [[Taming]] pet.
- The Owl Pet is the only pet intended not to have any stats.
## History

The Paladin Perfect Helmet Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that can be obtained from a [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Paladin Perfect Helmet Skin can be purchased from the Spring 2026 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it costs 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Paladin Perfect Helmet Skin can be applied to a [[Perfect Armor|Perfect Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 20 different versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 20 powerful paladin themed colors!

# Paragon Shard
Aliases: Paragon
Categories: Legendary items, Shards, Confirmations needed
[Paragon Shard]
Type: Shard ([[Attributes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Shard ID: L54
Families: None

Category: Combat
[Attribute]

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes
ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✕ No
Chameleon-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✕ No
[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: PARAGON
Tooltip: Slayer Discount I Grants a +0.5% discount on starting Slayer Quests.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-clickt to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 20.; LEGENDARY COMBAT SHARD (ID L54)
The Paragon Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Slayer Discount]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Paragon Shard can be rarely obtained from defeating any Tier IV and Tier V [[Slayer]] Boss excepted the [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]], provided the player is at least at [[Slayer|Slayer V (5)]] for the respective Slayer.
- The shard does not follow the usual weight-based system: instead, it uses a flat chance.
|Tier|Chance|Condition|
|-|-|-|
|Tier IV|0.25% (1 in 400 kills)|Not from [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] Slayer V (5) ([[Slayer]]) or above for the Slayer Boss slain.|
|Tier V|0.5% (1 in 200 kills)|Not from [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] Slayer V (5) ([[Slayer]]) or above for the Slayer Boss slain.|
- The shard does not belong to the Token, the Main or the Extra Loot Pool: instead, it rolls in a Separate Loot Pool in which it is the only drop. This means dropping it should[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] not prevent other shards such as the [[Primordial Shard]] from dropping as well.
- The [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meters]] can be set on it but will not increase its chance.
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Paragon Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Slayer Discount]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Slayer Discount ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +0.5%–5% discount on starting Slayer Quests ([[Slayer]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Paragon Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Paragon Shard has the following description:
- One always needs a bit of perfection to be the most powerful of one's kind.
## Bugs
- If obtained while non-[[Pity and RNG Meters|metered]] from anything other than the [[Voidgloom Seraph]] or the [[Inferno Demonlord]], both the chat message and SFX (Sound Effect) will not play.

The Pastel Ender Dragon Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Pastel Ender Dragon Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on June 30, 2021. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 7,500 purchases.

The Pastel Ender Dragon Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Ender Dragon Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

The Pearl Skeleton Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be obtained from a past [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Pearl Skeleton Skin could be purchased from the Fall 2024 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.

The Pearl Skeleton Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Skeleton Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

# Pearlescent Dye

[Pearlescent Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_PEARLESCENT
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #115555!; LEGENDARY DYE
Pearlescent Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
A Pearlescent Dye has a variable chance of dropping from all mobs in [[The End]]. The mobs affected, and associated drop chances, are:
Chance: Mobs
0.00001% (1 in 10,000,000): [[Enderman]] [[Endermite]] [[Obsidian Defender]] [[Seer]] [[Zealot]] [[Zealot Bruiser]]
0.00002% (1 in 5,000,000): [[Nest Endermite]] [[Voidling Extremist]] [[Voidling Fanatic]]
0.001% (1 in 100,000): [[End Stone Protector]] Old Dragon ([[Ender Dragon]]) Protector Dragon ([[Ender Dragon]]) Special Zealot ([[Zealot]]) Strong Dragon ([[Ender Dragon]]) Unstable Dragon ([[Ender Dragon]]) Wise Dragon ([[Ender Dragon]]) Young Dragon ([[Ender Dragon]])
0.002% (1 in 50,000): Superior Dragon ([[Ender Dragon]])
## Usage
Pearlescent Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #115555. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

Pendant of Divan is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Necklaces|Necklace]] obtained through Forging.
## Obtaining
Pendant of Divan can be forged in [[The Forge]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain X (10)]]. It takes 7 days to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Pendant of Divan]] 1 Pendant of Divan
  - 1 [[Shattered Locket]]

  - 10 [[Divan Fragment]]
## Usage
Necklace:
Pendant of Divan [[Cold Resistance]]: +10 [[Mining Speed]]: +100 [[Mining Fortune]]: +25
## Trivia
- Pendant of Divan is the only [[Equipment]] piece with [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]].
- Other Divan equipment pieces have been confirmed to be likely coming in the "far future".
  - Their existence in game code can be confirmed by providing item IDs to the (now removed) /wiki command: Divan Cloak (DIVAN_CLOAK), Divan Belt (DIVAN_BELT), and Divan Bracelet (DIVAN_BRACELET). However, these names are not final and subject to change.
- On December 18, 2024, 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Jayavarmen teased in a forum post that a fishing update is planned for the [[Great Glacite Lake]]. It will feature a new piece of the equipment set that the Pendant of Divan is part of.
  - In patch 0.24.2, it was teased that the next two pieces of Divan Equipment were coming as part of the [[Great Glacite Lake]] update.

The Penguin Hoodie Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Penguin Hoodie Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 7, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 2,000 purchases.

The Penguin Hoodie Skin can be applied to a [[Shark Scale Armor|Shark Scale Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 20 different versions.

Item ID: PERFECT_CHISEL
Tooltip: Gemstones: [❥] [❥] [❥]; Can be placed in the Fossil Excavator alongside a Suspicious Scrap to begin the excavation process!; Charges: 22; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 10.; LEGENDARY CHISEL
The Perfect Chisel is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Chisels|Chisel]].
## Obtaining
A Perfect Chisel can be forged at [[The Forge]] with 1 [[Glacite-Plated Chisel]], 1 [[Perfect Plate]] and 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain X (10)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.

## Usage
A Perfect Chisel can be used in the [[Fossil Excavator]] with [[Suspicious Scrap|Suspicious Scraps]] to find [[Fossils]] and other loot.
### Gemstones
Applying [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] will give different perks. Each Gemstone type grants a different buff to the Chisel, and is not affected by rarity, unlike all other instances of Gemstone being applied:
|Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Name|Bonus|Value by gemstone tier Rough|Value by gemstone tier Flawed|Value by gemstone tier Fine|Value by gemstone tier Flawless|Value by gemstone tier Perfect|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gemstone]]|Aquamarine|#% chance to gain an additional Chisel ([[Chisels]]) charge upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Citrine|#% chance to highlight a piece of treasure upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Onyx|#% chance to spawn an additional piece of treasure upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Peridot|Gain +#% Fossil Essence when using the the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|

A Perfect Plate is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
A Perfect Plate can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain X (10)]]. It requires 1 [[Umber Plate]], 1 [[Tungsten Plate]], 1 [[Mithril Plate]] and 30 minutes to forge.

# Perfectly Cut Diamond
Categories: Legendary items, Items
[Perfectly Cut Diamond]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[The Forge]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]

Buy: 1,000x (15 stacks plus 40 (1,000 in total)) [[Diamond Essence]] 2x [[Refined Diamond]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PERFECTLY_CUT_DIAMOND
Tooltip: An outstanding find. While few are known to exist, even fewer have been successfully retrieved.; Used in an anvil to upgrade Perfect Armor to Tier XIII.; LEGENDARY
The Perfectly Cut Diamond is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
The Perfectly Cut Diamond can be purchased from [[Gemma]] in the [[Crystal Nucleus]] for 1,000 (15 stacks plus 40) [[Diamond Essence]] and 2 [[Refined Diamond]].
## Usage
[[Perfectly Cut Diamond]] 1 Perfectly Cut Diamond can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with any [[Perfect Armor|Perfect Armor - Tier XII]] armor piece to upgrade it to Tier XIII. To fully upgrade a set of [[Perfect Armor|Perfect Armor - Tier XII]] to Tier XIII, one would need 4,000 (62 stacks plus 32) [[Diamond Essence]] and 8 [[Refined Diamond]].

|Oct 26, 2023|Fixed typo in lore.|

# Periwinkle Dye
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Periwinkle Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_PERIWINKLE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #CCCCFF!; LEGENDARY DYE
Periwinkle Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Periwinkle Dye has a chance to be obtained from slaying [[Runes|Runic Mobs]]. The obtainment chances scale based on the [[Mob|mob's]] Level.
|Mob Level|Drop Chance|
|-|-|
|1-99|0.0002% (1 in 500,000)|
|100-199|0.00025% (1 in 400,000)|
|200-299|0.0003% (1 in 300,000)|
|300-399|0.0005% (1 in 200,000)|
|400+|0.001% (1 in 100,000)|
## Usage
Periwinkle Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #CCCCFF. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
### Trivia
- Periwinkle Dye used to have a 1/100,000,000 chance to be found when [[Enchanting]] an item.
  - The chance increased by 5,000,000 for every 10 levels spent, up to 1/5,000,000.
- There are very few mobs with level 400 or higher.
  - [[Minos Inquisitor]] with level 750.
  - [[Lord Jawbus]] with level 600.
  - [[Thunder]] with lvl 400.
  - [[Magma Boss]] with lvl 500.
  - [[Arachne]] t2 with lvl 500.
  - [[Diamond Goblin]] with lvl 500.
  - Mobs in Infernal tier of [[Kuudra]] with lvl 500.
  - Mobs in Fiery tier of [[Kuudra]] with lvl 400.
## Gallery
- Periwinkle Dyed Leather Armor
- Periwinkle Bucket of Dye Periwinkle [[Bucket of Dye]]

|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Periwinkle Dye now drops from runic mobs.|
|Sep 11, 2024|Increased drop chance of Periwinkle Dye and created scale based on Mob Level.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

[Item Properties]

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 740,000x (11,562 stacks plus 32 (740,000 in total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]]) 36,960x (577 stacks plus 32 (36,960 in total)) [[Cobblestone]]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PERSONAL_COMPACTOR_7000
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +16; Automatically turns certain materials in your inventory into their enchanted form.; Right-click to configure!; Does not grant Carpentry XP!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; LEGENDARY ACCESSORY
Personal Compactor 7000 is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Redstone Dust|Redstone Dust IX (9)]]. Its ability turns certain materials in the inventory into their enchanted form similar to the [[Super Compactor 3000]]. This saves time crafting when grinding large quantities of items or collecting drops from [[Minions]]. It has 12 slots.
The auto-compaction abilities of different tiers of Personal Compactors work together with separate compacted items.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Redstone Dust IX (9) ([[Redstone Dust]])|1x [[Personal Compactor 6000]] 21x [[Enchanted Redstone Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] x3 ×7 + [[Personal Compactor 6000]] → Personal Compactor 7000|
## Tips
- It is recommended to get extra Personal Compactor 7000s for different sets of items, since players can use multiple personal compactors at the same time.
## Trivia
- [[Experience Artifact]], Personal Compactor, [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]], [[Book of Progression]], [[Melody's Hair]], [[Zombie Talisman]], [[Skeleton Talisman]], [[Archaeologist's Compass]], [[Lucky Hoof]], [[Eternal Hoof]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] and [[Ring of Love|Yellow Rock of Love]] are the only accessories to use a vanilla sprite, rather than a custom made one.

|Unknown Date|No longer gives [[Carpentry]] xp.|
|Sep 21, 2021|Items placed in the Personal Compactor 7000 are no longer placed inside, but a duplicate, unusable item is placed inside.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed Crafting Recipe from 56 Enchanted Redstone Blocks ([[Enchanted Redstone Block]]) and 1 [[Personal Compactor 6000]] to 21 Enchanted Redstone Blocks ([[Enchanted Redstone Block]]) and 1 [[Personal Compactor 6000]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed crafting requirement from Redstone Dust XIV (14) ([[Redstone Dust]]) to Redstone Dust IX (9) ([[Redstone Dust]]).|

[Upgrades]

Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No

Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 305,120x (4,767 stacks plus 32 (305,120 in total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 51,200x (800 stacks (51,200 items total)) [[Coal]] 25,600x (400 stacks (25,600 items total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
Raw Materials to upgrade: 179,200x (2,800 stacks (179,200 items total)) Iron ([[Iron Ingot]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PERSONAL_DELETOR_7000
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +16; Automatically delete specific items on pickup.; Right-click to configure!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; LEGENDARY ACCESSORY
The Personal Deletor 7000 is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingot XII (12)]] that automatically deletes certain materials in one's inventory when picked up. It is the upgrade of the [[Personal Deletor 6000]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Iron Ingot XII (12) ([[Iron Ingot]])|1x [[Personal Deletor 6000]] 7x [[Enchanted Iron Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×7 + [[Personal Deletor 6000]] → Personal Deletor 7000|
## Usage
Right-clicking the accessory while in the hand will open its configuration menu, where the player can place up to 12 items in the Auto-Deleting slot, similar to the [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactors]]. When the respective item enters the player's inventory, it will get deleted.
## Trivia
- The [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]] will only remove items from your inventory that you pick up off the ground. This is regardless of the [[Rarity]] of the item. However, it will not delete any item that does not have the same SkyBlock ID of the item in the deletor.
  - Example: You obtain a personal deletor and place a [[Golden Sword]] inside, any item you pick up with the SkyBlock ID of GOLDEN_SWORD will be deleted. The [[Midas' Sword]], which uses the Golden Sword item for its texture, will not be deleted upon pickup, as it does not have the SkyBlock ID of GOLDEN_SWORD.
- Unlike mentioned previously, when the Personal Deletors were first released, if players placed a vanilla iron sword in the GUI, it would also cause every in-game item with an iron sword texture (e.g. the [[Giant's Sword]] and [[Hyperion]]) to be deleted. This led to the Deletor being temporarily disabled.
  - However, certain items that share the same SkyBlock ID will still get deleted. For example, since all potions have a SkyBlock ID of POTION, putting any potion in a personal deletor would result in all potions being deleted.
    - This was partially patched in the 0.19.10 update by preventing potions from being placed in Personal Deletors.
- Secrets could be deleted without receiving credit for finding them, by putting a Secret item like a [[Decoy]] in a Personal Deletor and attempting to pick up the item.
  - This was later patched.
- [[Experience Artifact]], [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactor]], Personal Deletor, [[Book of Progression]], [[Melody's Hair]], [[Zombie Talisman]], [[Skeleton Talisman]], [[Archaeologist's Compass]], [[Lucky Hoof]], [[Eternal Hoof]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] and [[Ring of Love|Yellow Rock of Love]] are the only accessories to use a vanilla sprite, rather than a custom made one.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Added Personal Deletor 7000.|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Fixed Personal Deletor not working on loots from Frozen Treasures ([[Glacial Cave]]).|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed the Personal Deletor not deleting double catch items from [[Fishing]].|
|Aug 25, 2023|Fixed adding the same items to Personal Compactor/Deletor.|
|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Potions can no longer be placed in Personal Deletors.|

# Phantomleaf
Aliases: Phantom Leaf, Phantom leaf
Categories: Legendary items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Phantomleaf]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PHANTOMLEAF
Tooltip: It hides in plain sight, blending in with its surroundings until it's ready to reveal itself. If you listen closely, you can hear its pulse guiding you to the right spot.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; LEGENDARY MUTATION
A Phantomleaf is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Phantomleaf can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Chorus Fruit]] and 4 [[Shellfruit]] within 1 block of [[Soul Sand]].
While growing, a Phantomleaf does not need water and has 15 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it grants immunity to negative effects and increases [[Farming]] experience by 20%.
When harvested, a Phantomleaf hides in a random crop in the [[Greenhouse]] for 60 seconds. While hiding, a heart icon and heartbeat appear to aid in finding it. Clicking on the correct crop harvests the Phantomleaf. If the player clicks on the incorrect crop or time runs out, the Phantomleaf is destroyed.
If there are no other crops in the [[Greenhouse]], then the Phantomleaf will not hide, the minigame will not begin, and it will neither be harvested nor broken.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Phantomleafs also drop 3,000 (46 stacks plus 56) [[Potato]], 3,500 (54 stacks plus 44) [[Carrot]], and +8,000 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Phantomleaf can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 3,000,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 1,500 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Phantomleaf grants an additional 1,500 [[Copper]].
### NPC Trading
[[Phantomleaf]] 1 Phantomleaf is needed to purchase a [[Rose Dragon Pet|Rose Dragon Egg]] from [[Ludleth]].
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|While harvesting|[CROP] Phantomleaf: Poof! Try and find me!|
|While harvesting|[CROP] Phantomleaf: You have 45 seconds left!|
|While harvesting|[CROP] Phantomleaf: You have 30 seconds left!|
|While harvesting|[CROP] Phantomleaf: You have 15 seconds left!|
|While harvesting|[CROP] Phantomleaf: You have 5 seconds left!|
|Running out of time|[CROP] Phantomleaf: Time's up! You didn't find me fast enough!|
|Clicking incorrect crop|[CROP] Phantomleaf: That's not me! Better luck next time!|
|Clicking correct crop|[CROP] Phantomleaf: You found me!|
|No other crops planted|[CROP] Phantomleaf: I have no where to hide!|
## Gallery
- Phantomleaf in the Greenhouse Phantomleaf in the [[Greenhouse]]

|Dec 5, 2025|Added Phantomleaf.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Phantomleaf.|

Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: [[Stick]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PIGMAN_SWORD
Tooltip: Damage: +200; Strength: +100; Crit Chance: +5%; Crit Damage: +30%; Intelligence: +300; Ability: Burning Souls RIGHT CLICK Gain +75 Defense for 5s and cast vortex of flames towards enemies, dealing up to 30,000 over 5 seconds.; Mana Cost: 400 Cooldown: 5s; This item can be reforged!; LEGENDARY SWORD
The Pigman Sword is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]] unlocked at [[Raw Porkchop|Raw Porkchop IX (9)]]. Its ability summons flames to damage nearby mobs.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Porkchop IX (9) ([[Raw Porkchop]])|1x [[Stick]] 48x [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]] x24 ×2 + [[Stick]] → Pigman Sword|
## Usage
Sword:
Pigman Sword [[Damage]]: +200 [[Strength]]: +100 [[Crit Chance]]: +5 [[Crit Damage]]: +30 [[Intelligence]]: +300
[Effects]
[Ability: Burning Souls RIGHT CLICK; Gain +75 Defense for 5s and cast vortex of flames towards enemies, dealing up to 30,000 damage over 5 seconds.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 400; Cooldown: 5s; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.1]
When its ability is used, its user gains +75 [[Defense]] for 5 seconds. It also summons slow moving vortexes of flame to all nearby mobs. When one sticks to a mob, it deals 30,000 base ability damage over 5 seconds to it. The ability costs 400 Mana and has a cooldown of 5s.
The [[Pigman Pet]] buffs the Pigman Sword by up to 40 [[Damage]] and 25 [[Strength]].
## Essence Crafting
This weapon can be upgraded with Undead Essence. Once converted, it requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XVIII (18)]] to use.
Essence Required; [[Undead Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|200|200|
|✪|200|400|
|✪✪|300|700|
|✪✪✪|400|1,100|
|✪✪✪✪|500; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,600; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|600; 25,000 [[Coins]]|2,200; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- There is an [[Achievements|achievement]] named "This is Fair" that requires the player to kill a pig with the Pigman Sword.
- It is a reference to the Pigman Class from the Hypixel game mode [[References to other Hypixel minigames|Mega Walls]], which has an ability of the same name. However, in [[References to other Hypixel minigames|Mega Walls]], the ability grants strength instead of defense.
- If one were to put all 19,200 stacks of Porkchops to make the sword in double chests, it would take 712 double chests to store all of the Porkchops needed, or 4 large and 1 single chest of [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop|Enchanted Raw Porkchops]].
- All the Porkchops needed to craft the Pigman Sword will give the player approximately [[Farming]] 19.
- By a very small margin, [[Aspect of the Dragons]] outclasses the Pigman Sword.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PLASMAFLUX_POWER_ORB
Tooltip: Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK Place an orb for 1m buffing up to 5; players within 20 blocks.; Mana Cost: 50% of max Vitality Cost: 50; Orb Buff: Plasmaflux • Heal yourself for 60❤ per second.; • Heal others for 30❤ per second.; • Gain +125 Health Regen.; • Grants +125% base mana regen.; • Grants +35 Strength.; Only one deployable buff applies.; ☠ Requires Wolf Slayer 7.; LEGENDARY DEPLOYABLE
The Plasmaflux Power Orb is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Power Orbs|Power Orb]] usable after the player reaches [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer VIII (8)]]. It is an upgraded form of the [[Overflux Power Orb]], and is the final upgrade in the Orb series. Its requirement, the [[Plasma Nucleus]], can be purchased from the [[Dark Auction]] during the [[Dark Auction|Darker Auction]] Event when [[Scorpius]] is elected as mayor.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wolf Slayer LVL VIII (8) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x [[Overflux Power Orb]] 1x [[Plasma Nucleus]] 72x (1 stack plus 8 (72 in total)) [[Golden Tooth]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Enchanted Obsidian]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Obsidian]] x64 ×4 + [[Plasma Nucleus]] + [[Golden Tooth]] x24 ×3 + [[Overflux Power Orb]] → Plasmaflux Power Orb|
## Usage
Deployable:
Plasmaflux Power Orb
[Effects]
[Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK; Place an orb for 60s buffing up to 5 players within 18 blocks.; Mana Cost: 50% of max; Vitality Cost: 50 Vitality; Orb Buff: Plasmaflux; Heal yourself for 60 Health per second.; Heal others for 30 Health per second.; Gain +125 Health Regen.; Grants +125% base mana regen.; Grants +35 Strength.; Only one deployable buff applies.]
Using its ability does not remove or use up the item. When placed, it cannot be picked up; the ability spawns a new orb.
## Trivia
- Only 1 [[Deployables|Deployable]] can be placed at the same time (meaning a Plasmaflux Power Orb cannot be placed alongside an [[SOS Flare]]).
- Despite the crafting recipe being moved to [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer VIII (8)]], it can still be used at [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer VII (7)]]. This may be an oversight.
- It requires less [[Golden Tooth|Golden Teeth]] than its predecessor.

The Plushie Tuxedo Sloth Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Plushie Tuxedo Sloth Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on August 8, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 3,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Plushie Tuxedo Sloth Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Sloth Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 19 different versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 19 tuxedo-wearing plushie sloths!

Poignant Carrot Bait is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Consumables|Consumable]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Carrot Bait]] 32x [[Carrot Zest]]|Recipe: [[Carrot Zest]] x4 ×8 + [[Carrot Bait]] → Poignant Carrot Bait|
## Usage
Using a Poignant Carrot Bait on [[The Garden]] guarantees the next [[The Garden|Visitor]] to spawn to be the [[Carrot King Avatar]]. Doing so displays the following message:
The Carrot King Avatar acknowledges your presence. You hear a hopping noise getting nearer...
Using a Poignant Carrot Bait immediately after attracting the [[Carrot King Avatar]] will display the following message:
The Carrot King Avatar is already on their way...
If they are already in the queue, it will instead display:
The Carrot King Avatar does not take kindly to greed. Finish your active Carrot King Avatar commission first.

A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +13 Magic Find and +13 Overbloom.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; LEGENDARY PET ITEM
The Poignant Lucky Clover is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] obtained from the [[Carrot King Avatar]] that grants +13 [[Magic Find]] and +13 [[Overbloom]].
## Obtaining
### Garden Visitors
A Poignant Lucky Clover is rewarded from the [[Carrot King Avatar]] when summoned as a [[The Garden|Visitor]] on [[The Garden]] with [[Poignant Carrot Bait]]. The [[Carrot King Avatar]] requests a [[Lucky Clover]] and [[Enchanted Golden Carrot|Enchanted Golden Carrots]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Poignant Lucky Clover will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +13 [[Magic Find]] and +13 [[Overbloom]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Poignant Lucky Clover for 500,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.

The Pooch Sword is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer LVL VI (6)]] and is the upgraded version of the [[Shaman Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wolf Slayer VI (6) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x [[Shaman Sword]] 48x [[Golden Tooth]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] x24 ×2 + [[Shaman Sword]] → Pooch Sword|
## Usage
Sword:
Pooch Sword [[Damage]]: +160 [[Strength]]: +80 [[Attack Speed]]: +5% [[Speed]]: +10
[Effects]
[Deal +20 Damage per Wolf Slayer level.; Deal +200% Damage against Wolves.; Take -20% Damage from Animal mobs.; Gain +45 Combat Wisdom against Animal mobs. Ability: Pack Mentality; Deal +100% Damage against wolves when wearing a full set of Armor of the Pack or Mastiff Armor]
The Pooch Sword deals +20 [[Damage]] per [[Wolf Slayer]] level, alongside granting +200% [[Damage]] against wolves and +45 [[Combat Wisdom]] against [[Animal]] mobs. Additionally, while holding the Pooch Sword, the wielder receives -20% damage from [[Animal]] mobs.
## Tips
- The Pooch Sword pairs well with [[Armor of the Pack]] and [[Mastiff Armor]] due to the large [[Damage]] bonus against wolves given by the armor sets.

# Postcard

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POSTCARD
Tooltip: When placed on your island, it will inspire your minions with the belief that they too, might one day be given the choice to leave if they work hard enough.; All minions on your island will work 5%; faster.; Your [minion] [tier] sent you a stunning image featuring a towering mountain on a very, very distant island.; LEGENDARY
A Postcard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
A Postcard is sent to [[Jerry]] when a minion chooses to leave when given [[Free Will]].
## Usage
A Postcard can be placed on the [[Private Island]] to grant all [[Minions]] a +5% boost to speed. Only 1 Postcard can be placed per [[Private Island]].

## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chest
A Power Dragon Shard can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in the [[Catacombs]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Power Dragon Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Elite]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Elite ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against bosses and mini-bosses.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

A Power Dragon Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Power Dragon Shard has the following description:
- Power's roars shake the very earth, its fiery breath capable of melting the toughest armor and incinerating anything in its path.
## Trivia
- Despite being a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Power Dragon Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

Tooltip: Power Stone Combine x9 of this item at the Thaumaturgist in the Hub to unlock the Pleasant power.; At 1,000 Magical Power:; +434.27&#xE010; Health +465.29&#xE008; Defense; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 25.; EPIC POWER STONE
The Precious Pearl is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Pleasant]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Health]] and [[Defense]].
## Obtaining
Precious Pearl has a 0.164% (0.1638028%) (approximately 1/610) chance per roll to drop from the [[Crystal Nucleus]] loot bundle. The average chance to obtain at least one is 2.75% (approximately 1/36) per nucleus run.
## Requirements
The Precious Pearl has a [[Combat|Combat XXV (25)]] requirement and is used as a [[Power Stones|Power Stone]] for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Precious Pearls at [[Maxwell]] will unlock the Pleasant power permanently.

## Obtaining
A Precursor Drone Pet can be forged in [[The Forge]] with 1 [[Precursor Apparatus]] and 300,000 [[Coins]] for 3 days. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]] to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Precursor Drone Pet]] 1 Precursor Drone Pet
  - 1 [[Precursor Apparatus]] 1 [[Control Switch]] 1 [[Electron Transmitter]] 1 [[FTX 3070]] 1 [[Robotron Reflector]] 1 [[Superlite Motor]] 1 [[Synthetic Heart]]
  - 300,000 (4,687 stacks plus 32) [[Coins]]

## Usage
The Precursor Drone Pet can have up to 2 [[Drone Mods]] applied to grant it abilities, trying to apply a 3rd Drone Mod will require replacing one of the current ones.
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Pastel Precursor Drone Skin]]|[[Pastel Precursor Drone Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Event Shop]] - Easter 2026 - 100 Event Silver|
## Tips
- [[Drone Mod: Mining Off Camera]] can greatly increase profits from items with a low value.
  - When mining [[Cobblestone]], the pet increases profit by 4.39%, not including the 75 [[Mining Fortune]] or [[Pet Items]].
  - When mining [[Hard Stone]], the pet increases profit by 14.178%, not including the 75 [[Mining Fortune]] or [[Pet Items]].
  - When mining [[Sand]], the pet increases profit by 3.011%, not including the 75 [[Mining Fortune]] or [[Pet Items]].
  - When mining [[Netherrack]], the pet increases profit by 13.994%, not including the 75 [[Mining Fortune]] or [[Pet Items]].
- With 100 [[Sea Creature Chance]], [[Drone Mod: Contraband]] acts like an additional 10 [[Treasure Chance]] on top of the sea creatures.
  - Rolled [[Treasure]] is affected by [[Treasure|GREAT CATCH]] or [[Treasure|OUTSTANDING CATCH]] buffs, like [[Blessed Bait]].
- Since the Precursor Drone is an [[Pets|All Skills Pet]], it can be leveled quickly with [[Enchanting|Enchanting XP]].
## Bugs
- Despite not having a [[Drone Mod: Contraband]] applied, the player can still catch [[Treasure]] alongside [[Sea Creatures]].
## Trivia
- The Precursor Drone Pet was created by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] thirtyvirus after becoming a [[Minister Election|Minister]] for [[Candidate Aura|Aura]].
- The [[Drone Mod: Mining Off Camera]] makes 0.0388 [[Coins]] to NPC, or 0.1402 [[Coins]] to the [[Bazaar]] (sell offer) per collection gain on average.

The Precursor Eye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Helmets|Helmet]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Precursor Eye can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]])|Bedrock|30,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.1125% (1 in 888.7 runs)|0.1125% (1 in 888.7 runs)|370|3|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|30,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.1108% (1 in 902.3 runs)|0.1983% (1 in 504.4 runs)|370|3|
## Usage
Helmet:
Precursor Eye [[Health]]: +222 [[Defense]]: +222 [[Intelligence]]: +222
[Effects]
[Ability: Eye Beam SNEAK; Fire a laser in front of you dealing 4000 damage and costing 40 mana. The damage increases by 100% every second for 5 seconds and the mana cost increases by 25% every second. You can sneak again to de-activate the laser.]
The Precursor Eye's main ability, Eye Beam, can be toggled upon sneaking: this summons a laser that travels outwards from the user's head, which has a range of 30 blocks. This laser deals 4,000 damage to enemies in its path, costing 40 [[Mana]] per second to fire. Toggling the laser while it is active disables the laser, resetting its damage and [[Mana]] cost.
[[Intelligence]] and [[Ability Damage]] do not work with the Eye Beam ability.

|[[Oculus Skin]]|[[Oculus Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - December 6, 2025 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Trivia
- The Precursor Eye is the only armor piece that can be enchanted with [[Ultimate Wise]].
- Holding any [[Weapons|Weapon]] with [[Ultimate Wise]] decreases the [[Mana]] cost for the laser. Similarly, the [[Sheep Pet]] and the [[Attributes|Breeze]] attribute also decrease the [[Mana]] cost in relation to their levels.
  - Holding a weapon with enchantments that affect magic damage, such as [[Smite]] also applies to the laser damage.
- The Precursor Eye is the most expensive item that can be unlocked from Dungeon Reward Chests in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].

A Prickly Creeper is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrade]] that can be applied to any [[Armor]] with [[Thorns|Thorns III]] to upgrade it to [[Thorns|Thorns IV]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|7x [[Enchanted Cactus]] 16x [[Cactus Flower]] 64x [[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|Recipe: [[Cactus Flower]] x16 + [[Enchanted Cactus]] ×7 + [[Enchanted Gunpowder]] x64 → Prickly Creeper|
## Usage
A Prickly Creeper can be applied in an anvil to any [[Armor]] with [[Thorns|Thorns III]] to upgrade it to [[Thorns|Thorns IV]].
## Tips
- It is recommended to apply a Prickly Creeper to [[Helianthus Armor]], or whichever set of armor you use for farming, if you wish to maximise the bonus of [[Reforging|Thorny]], which grants +0.1 [[Overbloom]] per level of [[Thorns]] on your armor - up to +1.6 [[Overbloom]] with [[Thorns|Thorns IV]] on all 4 pieces of armor.

# Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum
Aliases: Nex, Prime Huntaxe, Prime Huntaxe Nex Titanum

Stats: [[Damage]]: +25
[Upgrades]

Next →: None
[Item Properties]

Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+15 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NEX_TITANUM
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Damage: +25; Ability: Absorptio RIGHT CLICK This weapon lets you absorb 25% of the basic combat stats of any weapon.; Ability: Vis Temperata Each strike of this weapon has its damage capped at 50% of the enemy's max Health. This weapon cannot cause a fatal blow.; Titanum ruina, mundi mutatio.; This item can be reforged!; LEGENDARY AXE
The Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Huntaxes|Huntaxe]] crafted at [[Vinesap|Vinesap IX (9)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vinesap IX (9) ([[Vinesap]])|1x [[Savage Huntaxe - Praedator]] 6x [[Enchanted Vinesap]] 64x [[Stretching Sticks]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Vinesap]] x2 ×3 + [[Savage Huntaxe - Praedator]] + [[Stretching Sticks]] x64 → Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum|
## Usage
The Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum has two abilities:
- Absorptio allows the Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum to absorb 25% of the basic [[Stats|Combat Stats]] ([[Damage]], [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]], etc) of any weapon.
- Vis Temperata caps the [[Damage]] of the Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum at 50% of the enemy's max health and prevents the user from killing the enemy. This allows one to easily syphon their [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] with a [[Small Pocket Black Hole|Small]] or [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]].
## Trivia
- The text in the Huntaxe's description, "Titanum ruina, mundi mutatio." is Latin, and means "Fall of Titans, the change of the world."
  - The item's name, "Nex Titanum", means "Death of the Titans", and likely refers to the disappearance of the Starborns.
  - The ability name "Vis Temperata" means "Tempered force".

Primordial Armor's Octodexterity tiered bonus increases the [[Damage]] of every third hit by +50% and applies the Venom effect, reducing healing by 10% for 4s. Additionally, each piece's Spider Bulwark bonus grants [[Defense]] against Spiders.

Additionally, each piece of Primordial Armor has a unique piece bonus:
- The [[Primordial Armor|Primordial Helmet]]'s Radioactive perk grants +1.5 [[Crit Damage]] per 10 [[Strength]], up to +150 [[Crit Damage]] at 1000 [[Strength]].
- The [[Primordial Armor|Primordial Chestplate]]'s Anti-Toxin perk reduces the effectiveness of [[Tarantula Broodfather]]'s [[Vitality]] reduction by 50%.
- The [[Primordial Armor|Primordial Leggings]]' Brood perk increases the base stats of the [[Tarantula Pet]] by 100%.
  - This comes out to 10 [[Strength]], 10 [[Crit Chance]], and 30 [[Crit Damage]] if the [[Tarantula Pet]] is level 100.
- The [[Primordial Armor|Primordial Boots]]' Double Jump ability allows the player to double jump in mid-air, costing 20 [[Mana]].

## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Tier V [[Tarantula Broodfather]] has a 0.014% (1 in 7,026.5 kills) to drop a Primordial Eye if the player has reached [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer VII (7)]].
### Raffle of the Century
Primordial Eye can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].

The Primordial Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Cocoon Chance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Primordial Shard has a base 0.71% (1 in 141.53 kills) chance to drop from defeating a Tier V [[Tarantula Broodfather|Primordial Broodfather]].
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Primordial Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Cocoon Chance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Cocoon Chance ([[Attributes]]); Increases the chance of [[The Primordial]] cocooning Slayer Bosses ([[Slayer]]) and Slayer Minibosses ([[Minibosses]]) by +0.5%–5%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Protector Dragon Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set that is focused on [[Defense]]. Its Full Set Bonus grants extra [[Defense]] as [[Health]] is lost. The full set can be crafted from 240 (3 stacks plus 48) [[Dragon Fragment|Protector Dragon Fragments]] and is one of 8 different [[Dragon Armor]] sets.
[[Combat|Combat XVI (16)]] (XVI) is required to be able to use this set and, if a dungeon item, [[Catacombs|Catacombs XII (12)]] (XII) to be able to use this set while in [[Dungeons]].

## Tips
- If one wants to complement its stat specialty, the [[Reforging|Titanic]], [[Reforging|Reinforced]], [[Reforging|Perfect]] or [[Reforging|Giant]] [[Reforging|reforge]] is recommended to get [[Health]] and/or [[Defense]].
- Protector Dragon Armor is considered by many as the worst out of the [[Dragon Armor]] sets, as it performs poorly for damage and provides less health compared to other Dragon sets.
- Players will find Holy Dragon Armor to be the better choice instead, as Holy Dragon Armor provides superior stat boosts and is much cheaper.
## Trivia
- The [[Effective Health]] of this armor depends on the amount of damage taken at once, as the [[Defense]] bonus is calculated based on the instantaneous [[Health]] remaining.
- When the player is on very low [[Health]], this armor set provides almost as much [[Defense]] as Tier X [[Perfect Armor]].

Tooltip: Ability: Puzzle RIGHT CLICK Lets you solve a puzzle.; Puzzles Solved: 0; LEGENDARY
The Puzzle Cube is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item. It displays the buyer's name, cost paid, and the details about in which auction it was sold and the ranking of the buyer within that auction. This data disappears if the item is traded in any way.
## Obtaining
The Puzzle Cube is one of the five items that appear at [[Shen's Auction|Shen's Special]] while [[Diaz]] is elected with her Volume Trading perk and can be bid on by players. The top 20 bidders win the item. [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profiles have a separate auction, where top 10 bidders get the item.
## Usage
The Puzzle Cube's Puzzle ability can be used to start a random [[Terminal]] from [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]. Its tooltip tracks the number of puzzles solved.

The Pyrochaos Dagger is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer VI (6)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer VI (6) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Kindlebane Dagger]] 8x [[Gold Magmafish]] 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 64x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Gold Magmafish]] x8 + [[Molten Powder]] x64 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + [[Kindlebane Dagger]] → Pyrochaos Dagger|
## Usage
Sword:
Pyrochaos Dagger [[Damage]]: +160 [[Strength]]: +75 [[Crit Chance]]: +10 [[Crit Damage]]: +25 [[Attack Speed]]: +20
[Effects]
[Deal 2x damage to Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs.; Deal 1.5x damage to Wither mobs.; Gain +45 Combat Wisdom against Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs. Ability: Attunement ON RIGHT CLICK; Swap between Ashen and Auric mode in order to attack through Hellion Shields. Ability: Burning Vengeance ﬗ; On hit, repeat the last 6 hits of dealt damage after 6s if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement.]
The Pyrochaos Dagger deals 2x damage to [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs and 1.5x damage to [[Skeletal]] mobs. Additionally, it provides +45 [[Combat Wisdom]] against [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs.
The Pyrochaos Dagger has 2 abilities:
- Attunement allows the wielder to swap between Ashen and Auric attunements in order to attack through the [[Inferno Demonlord]]'s Hellion Shield ability. When switching attunement, the Pyrochaos Dagger changes appearance from a Iron Sword to a Golden Sword.
- Burning Vengeance allows the wielder to repeat the last 6 hits of dealt damage after 6s upon hit if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement. This does not affect the damage immunity of demons, and has a cap of 25,000,000 [[Damage]].

A Quick Claw is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] obtained from the [[Crystal Nucleus]]. It increases the player's [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]] for every 2 [[Pets|Pet]] levels.
## Obtaining
### Crystal Nucleus
A Quick Claw has a 0.01649% (1 in 6064.9 rolls) chance per roll to appear in a [[Crystal Nucleus]] loot bundle.
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Quick Claw will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +1 [[Mining Fortune]] and +1 [[Mining Speed]] for every 2 levels the [[Pets|Pet]] has. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Quick Claw for 500,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.

Tooltip: Miracle Chance I Increases your chance of finding Dyes by +0.1%.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 20.; LEGENDARY FOREST SHARD (ID L49)
The Rainbug Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Miracle Chance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Safari Bells
The Rainbug Shard cannot be obtained as an individual [[Attributes|Shard]]. Instead, its [[Attributes|Miracle Chance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] can be obtained and leveled up by completing a quest in the [[Critter Safari]].
There are 7 Bells scattered around the [[Critter Safari]] at different locations. Upon finding all 7 Bells and returning to [["Hunter" Tobias]], a cutscene begins to play. For the sake of spoilers, the following section will be hidden.
Show/Hide Bell Coordinates
- [[Coordinates|-68, 66, -43]]: A Bell is hidden on the bottom of an ice platform near the entrance to the Icy biome. Jumping on the platform repeatedly will cause it to flip over, yielding the [[Achievements|What the flip?!]] secret achievement. The bell will be too cold to ring, requiring an [[Icebreaker]] to break it.
- [[Coordinates|-96, 46, -57]]: A Bell is hidden behind a bed found in an underground cavern in the underwater lake in the Icy biome.
- [[Coordinates|-4, 96, -42]]: A Bell is hidden on the roof of the mansion in the Haunted biome. It can be accessed from parkour from the bottom of the mansion, or from climbing to the top of the Icy biome and jumping into the roof.
- [[Coordinates|47, 55, -7]]: A Bell is hidden at the edge of the Critter Safari, accessed from the Forest biome waterfalls, before dropping down and walking towards the Haunted biome while on the outer rim of the Critter Safari. The ledge can be exited safely by ascending from the vine at [[Coordinates|16, 53, 79]].
- [[Coordinates|-30, 125, 59]]: A Bell is hidden near the top of the Forest biome, requiring parkour across a large tree to a floating island.
- [[Coordinates|-90, 109, 16]]: A Bell is hidden at the top of the Cavern biome: you will need to climb from a fake cactus onto a large stack from [[Coordinates|-138, 102, 26]], parkour across a tree, and make it to another stack where the bell is found.
- [[Coordinates|-50, 81, 0]]: A Bell is hidden at the top of the landing zone, accessible from an ice spike by the entrance to the Icy biome.
Each bell corresponds to a specific color in the rainbow. Completing the rainbow and returning to the landing zone yields the following dialogue from [["Hunter" Tobias]]:
[NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: Uh guys, you better come and see this...
Finally, a cutscene plays as the player walks into the landing zone. As the player looks at the rainbow, the following dialogue plays:
Wow, a rainbow. I haven't seen one of them in a long time. Isn't it beautiful? You know, there are so many memories in a rainbow. I remember seeing one while playing with all my friends, back when we were very small. The next time I saw one was many years later, after one of them died. Sometimes it feels like, every time I see one, there are less of my kind left to see it with me. But it's still oddly comforting. Before I was here, there were rainbows. After I am gone, there will still be rainbows. Throughout my short life, everything I once knew has changed beyond recognition. As for the rainbows, I don't think they notice the difference.
With each dialogue, the player's [[Attributes|Miracle Chance]] attribute gains +1 level, maxing out upon the cutscene ending. Players also receive [[Achievements|The Miracle in a sunbeam]] secret achievement.
During this cutscene, a custom [[Hypixel SkyBlock OST|song]] plays:
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Rainbug Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Miracle Chance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Miracle Chance ([[Attributes]]); Increases your chance of finding Dyes by 0.1%–1%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
