# Ray of Helios
Aliases: Helios, Ray of
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Ray of Helios]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: A waning shard of sunlight, as though torn from a forgotten age.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RAY_OF_HELIOS
Tooltip: A waning shard of sunlight, as though torn from a forgotten age.; Right-click to view recipes!; LEGENDARY
The Ray of Helios is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item rarely dropped from [[Harvest Feast]] crops. It is used to craft the [[Sun's Grasp]].
## Obtaining
A Ray of Helios has a 0.0000002% (1 in 500,000,000 crops broken) chance to be obtained from harvesting in-season crops during the [[Harvest Feast]].
[[Overbloom]] can be used to increase the chance of finding Ray of Helios.
A Ray of Helios could also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from the [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sun's Grasp]]|None|[[Ray of Helios]] 1x Ray of Helios 3x [[Godseed]] 3x [[All-in Aloe]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Compacted Sunflower]]|Recipe: [[Godseed]] ×3 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x64 ×2 + Ray of Helios + [[All-in Aloe]] ×3 → [[Sun's Grasp]]|
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Announcement|----------------------------------------------------- CRAZY RARE DROP! <player> just found a Ray of Helios! -----------------------------------------------------|
## Trivia
- The reasoning for Ray of Helios's incredibly low drop chance and the reason why the [[Sun's Grasp]] is not present in the [[Museum]] is outlined by 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] MotorGorilla :
[…] Pretty much everything rare in the game is now tied to an RNG Meter or Pity which largely takes away a lot of the surprise of dropping something super rare. Everything being tied to SB XP also makes people feel like they need to get something. […] The idea here was to make an item that not everyone will be able to get that is not needed for progression that still does something kinda cool white not being all that good. […] It's entirely optional, and it's intended to bring back a little bit of the excitement from super rare drops pre-RNG Meter days.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 31, 2026|Added Ray of Helios.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Added Ray of Helios.|

## Usage
Armor Set:
|Chestplate|Leggings|
|-|-|
|Reaper Chestplate ([[Reaper Armor]]); [[Health]]: +180 [[Defense]]: +70 [[Strength]]: +25 [[Intelligence]]: +250 Piece Bonus: Zombie Bulwark; Kill Zombies to accumulate defense against them.|Reaper Leggings ([[Reaper Armor]]); [[Health]]: +120 [[Defense]]: +50 [[Strength]]: +25 [[Intelligence]]: +250 Piece Bonus: Zombie Bulwark; Kill Zombies to accumulate defense against them.|
|Boots||

[Total]
[Reaper Armor Health: +400 Defense: +150 Strength: +75 Intelligence: +750]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Enrage SNEAK; Enrage for 6s gaining 100Speed, 100Damage, and 100Strength.; Cooldown: 25s Full Set Bonus: Trolling the Reaper; Healing Wands heal +50%.; Gain +100 Defense against Undead (Undead (Mob Type)) mobs.; Deal +100% damage to Undead (Undead (Mob Type)) mobs but 1% to all other Mobs.]
Reaper Armor has 2 full set bonuses: Enrage and Trolling the Reaper.
- Trolling the Reaper increases healing from the [[Wand of Healing]], [[Wand of Mending]], [[Wand of Restoration]], and the [[Wand of Atonement]] by 50%. Additionally, it grants +100 [[Defense]] against [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs and increases damage dealt to [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs by +100%. However, it sets the player's [[Damage]] against non-[[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs to 1%.
- Enrage is an ability activated on SNEAK. It grants +100 [[Speed]], +100 [[Damage]], and +100 [[Strength]] for 6s. Switching to a different armor set will cancel its effects.
Additionally, each piece has a Zombie Bulwark full set bonus, which grants [[Defense]] against Zombies.
The [[Wands|Healing Wand]] bonus requires the set to have been worn for at least 3 seconds to activate.

|12|100000|+300 [[Defense]]|

|14|500000|+315 [[Defense]]|
## Trivia
- There is no designated helmet to Reaper Armor, due to [[Zombie Slayer]] pushing the player to use the [[Warden Helmet]] or [[Reaper Mask]] alongside it.

# Reaper Gem
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Pet items
[Reaper Gem]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Maddox the Slayer]]
Uses: Gain 8 Fero ([[Ferocity]]) for 5s on kill.
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Zombie Slayer IX (9) ([[Zombie Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REAPER_GEM
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Gain 8 Ferocity for 5s on kill.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; * Co-op Soulbound * LEGENDARY PET ITEM
The Reaper Gem is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Reaper Gem will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it 8 [[Ferocity]] for 5s on kill. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Reaper Gem for 500,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## Obtaining
After reaching [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer IX (9)]], [[Maddox the Slayer]] will reward the [[Player]] with a Reaper Gem. It can be re-purchased from [[Rusty]] for 20,000,000 [[Coins]] once it has been claimed.
## Trivia
- The Energized Chunk is a recolored texture of this item.
  - The same is true of the [[Shy Crux]], [[Shadow Crux]], [[Volt Crux]], [[Scribe Crux]], [[Frosty Crux]], [[Splatter Crux]], and [[Puff Crux]].
- The Reaper Gem is the only [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that is [[Soulbound]].
- Only one could be obtained per [[Profile]] before [[Rusty]] sold back one-time items.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Mar 12, 2021|Added Reaper Gem.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Reaper Gem is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Apr 25, 2022|Reaper Gem can now be purchased from [[Rusty]] if the [[Player]] has already claimed one.|

# Reaper Pepper
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Permanent Consumables
[Reaper Pepper]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REAPER_PEPPER
Tooltip: Consume this dangerous pepper to permanently gain +1 Health and +1 Cold Resistance.; You may eat up to 5 peppers before it's considered truly dangerous.; LEGENDARY
The Reaper Pepper is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Consumables|Permanent Consumable]] item used to increase the player's max [[Health]] and [[Cold Resistance]].
## Obtaining
A Reaper Pepper has a 0.0002182539% (1/458,182) chance to be generated per item harvested by an [[Inferno Minion]] while it is using [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] (Hypergolic) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]].
## Usage
Up to 5 Reaper Peppers can be consumed to permanently gain +1 [[Health]], +1 [[Cold Resistance]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|10 SkyBlock XP]] per Pepper.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|First Pepper|You've eaten a reaper pepper, it's delicious!|
|Second Pepper|You've eaten a reaper pepper, it's uh... really hot.|
|Third Pepper|You've eaten a reaper pepper and begun regretting this decision.|
|Fourth Pepper|You've eaten a reaper pepper, where is the MILK??|
|Fifth Pepper|You've eaten a reaper pepper, you feel death creeping up in your spine. Your whole life flashes by. Is this what it means to be endgame?|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|You already ate the maximum number of reaper peppers! You may want to visit a hospital ASAP.|
## Trivia
- The Reaper Pepper is likely a reference to the Carolina Reaper, the world's second-hottest pepper according to the Guinness World Records, measuring up to 2.2 million Scoville heat units.
## History

|Apr 24, 2024|0.20.1|Reaper Pepper now gives permanent [[Cold Resistance]].|

The Reaper Scythe is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Zombie Slayer VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Scythe Blade]] 3x [[Reaper Falchion]] 64x [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Scythe Blade]] + [[Revenant Viscera]] x64 + [[Reaper Falchion]] ×3 → Reaper Scythe|
## Usage
Sword:
Reaper Scythe [[Damage]]: +333 [[Speed]]: +10
[Effects]
[Ability: Raise Souls RIGHT CLICK; Monsters you kill using this item will drop their soul. You can click on their souls on the ground using this item to absorb them and then spawn them to fight by your side.; Mana cost is based on the power of the monsters you summon. Shift right-click to view and remove souls from this item. If your summoned monster dies the soul will be removed.; Cooldown: 1s; Max Souls (Necromancy): 3]
The Reaper Scythe grants a chance to drop a mob's soul on kill. Clicking on their souls on the ground while holding a Reaper Scythe allows the player to absorb and spawn them.
The Reaper Scythe's Raise Souls ability allows the player to summon collected souls at the cost of [[Mana]], with the [[Mana]] cost increasing with the mob's level. The soul is removed upon the summoned mob's death.
The following items will buff the Reaper Scythe:
- The [[Reaper Mask]] allows the wearer to store and summon 2 extra Necromancy mobs.
- The [[Ghoul Pet]]'s Army of the Dead ability increases the amount of souls that the player can summon by 2 and increases the chances of collecting a soul by +20%.
  - The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Ghoul Pet]]'s Reaper Soul reduces the [[Mana]] costs of summoning mobs by 30%, increases their damage by +20%, and increases their health by +100%.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Undead Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|200|200|
|✪✪|300|500|
|✪✪✪|400|900|
|✪✪✪✪|500; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,400; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|600; 25,000 [[Coins]]|2,000; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- Summoning a Necromancy mob will heal them back to full health. This can be used in [[Enderman Slayer]]: the [[Voidgloom Seraph]] despawns summoned mobs, allowing players to store mobs such as [[Wither Spectre|Wither Spectres]] or [[Tank Zombie|Tank Zombies]] to fight them.

# Recombobulator 3000
Aliases: Recomb, Recombobulator
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Catacombs Loot, Confirmations needed, Catacombs, Dungeons, Dungeon Items
[Recombobulator 3000]
Type: Dungeon Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RECOMBOBULATOR_3000
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Permanently increases the rarity of an item. Can only be applied to an item once.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON ITEM
The Recombobulator 3000 is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon item]] that can be used to upgrade the [[Rarity]] of most items by one tier.
## Obtaining
### Boss Collections
The Recombobulator 3000 can be obtained from [[Collections|Collection]] rewards obtained by killing any [[Dungeons]] boss 250 times, excluding [[Kuudra]].
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Recombobulator 3000 can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor I]] through [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|VII]].

## Usage
The Recombobulator 3000 can be used in an [[Anvil]] to upgrade the [[Rarity]] of any item, although it can only be used once on each item, thus only allowing for a single rarity increase. This is not counted as a [[Reforging|Reforge]]. Reforges can be applied before or afterwards as normal, but still get boosted by the rarity upgrade.
It is not possible to recombobulate an already recombobulated item obtained through the [[Auto Recombobulator]] accessory, because when the [[Auto Recombobulator]]'s effect comes into play, it will count as if a Recombobulator 3000 has been applied.
Increasing rarity using a Recombobulator 3000 on a rarity will have the following effect:
|From|To|Notes|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])||
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|RARE ([[Rarity]])||
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|EPIC ([[Rarity]])||
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])||
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])||
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|The only items with an intentional base MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) rarity are [[Divan's Drill]], [[Cake Soul]], [[David's Cloak]], [[Scorched Books]], [[Diamond Magmafish]], [[Complete Century Cake Bundle]], [[Plasma Bucket]], a selection of MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) pet upgrades, [[Dyes]], and Rift items.|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|The only item with a DIVINE ([[Rarity]]) base rarity is the [[Celestial Starstone]]. It can only be recombobulated using [[Centauri]] on AHN ([[Alpha Hypixel Network]]).|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])||
|VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|There are no known items with a VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]) base rarity.|
|ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])|VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|This rarity is only used for cosmetics and these items cannot be recombobulated, except using [[Centauri]] on AHN ([[Alpha Hypixel Network]]).|
## Trivia
- Items that have had a Recombobulator 3000 applied to them are said to have been Recombobulated.
- Increasing the rarity of items increases the effect of [[Reforging|Reforges]] and [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] applied to the item.
- [[Pets]] and [[Pet Items]] cannot be Recombobulated, but most pets can have their rarity increased by [[Kat]] or by using the [[Tier Boost]] pet item. Some pets cannot have their rarity upgraded through traditional means.
  - Despite this, using [[Centauri]] to Max Items in players inventory will lead to all items that cannot be Recombobulated to display the same effect as if they were, without changing the rarity.
- [[Rod Parts]] and [[Reforge Stones]] cannot be Recombobulated because their apply cost is based on their rarity.
- The Recombobulator 3000, prior to the 0.18.5 patch, could be bought in Obsidian and Bedrock post-boss chests for only 5,000,000 [[Coins]].
- Applying a Recombobulator 3000 to an item for the first time gives you the [[Achievements|Upgrades people, Upgrades!]] achievement.
  - The name of this achievement is likely a reference to a Phineas T. Ratchet quote from the 2005 animated movie Robots.
- If you recombobulate an item and then insert it into your sacks or sell it on the [[Bazaar]], it will return to its original rarity, as those don't preserve NBT data.
## History

|May 17, 2020|First tease of Recombobulator 3000 by Jayavarmen.|
|Jul 1, 2020|0.7.11|Added Recombobulator 3000. The item is unobtainable and can only be viewed in [[Bazaar]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Recombobulator 3000 can now be obtained through Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]).|
|Oct 20, 2021|Removed the ability to recombobulate items that are not Armor, Weapons, Tools or Accessories|
|Dec 20, 2021|Reverted previous changes; Re-added the ability to Recombobulate items that aren't Armor, Weapons, Tools or Accessories.|
|May 31, 2023|0.18.5|Raised the drop chances of the Recombobulator 3000 in most floors.|
|May 31, 2023|0.18.5|Increased the chest price by 1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|May 31, 2023|0.18.5|Floor 1 and Floor 2 Recombobulator 3000 prices are now adjusted to be the same as on the other floors.|

Refined Titanium is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
Refined Titanium can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It requires 16 [[Enchanted Titanium]] and 12 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Refined Titanium]] 1 Refined Titanium
  - 16 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Titanium]]

# Revive Stone
Aliases: Reviving Stone
Categories: Legendary items, Items, More info needed, Dungeons
[Revive Stone]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REVIVE_STONE
Tooltip: Ability: Revive RIGHT CLICK Revive a player who has died in a dungeon.; ONE TIME USE.; This item will vanish from your inventory at the end of the Dungeon!; LEGENDARY DUNGEON ITEM
A Revive Stone is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeon Items|Dungeon Item]] that can revive dead players when in a [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] run.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Revive Stone has a 100% (1 in 1) to drop from killing a [[Fairy]] while all the player's teammates are alive.
There is a 22% (1 in 4.5) chance for one drop from killing a [[Crypt Undead]], as well as an additional 100% (1 in 1) chance for one to drop from killing a [[Prince]] version of one.
There is also a small chance[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) for one to appear when killing every starred mob in a room.
### Shops
[[Winona]] sells Revive Stones in the [[Catacombs]] for 100,000 [[Coins]] each.
Additionally, the [[Essence Shops|Undead Essence Shop]] contains the Help of the Fairies perk, which grants a free Revive Stone at the start of all Dungeon runs.
## Usage
Item:
Revive Stone
[Effects]
[Ability: Revive RIGHT CLICK; Revive a player who has died in a dungeon. ONE TIME USE.; This item will disappear from your inventory at the end of the Dungeon!]
Revive Stone's main purpose is to revive dead players in Dungeons. If a player with a Revive Stone dies, it is consumed in order to revive them at their location.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Which takes priority? Healer passive, Help of the Fairies or player's Revive Stone? Does the player get revived at the respective location?; When this info is added, remove this.). It is also possible to revive other players using it. If only one player is dead, right-clicking a Revive Stone consumes the item to revive them. If there are multiple, it opens a menu, where the player can choose which deceased player to revive, after which the item is consumed. The reviving process itself takes 5 seconds.
### Abiphone Contacts
Players must give [[Revive Stone]] 2 Revive Stones to obtain the [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact for [[Trinity]].
## Game Messages
|Message|Condition|
|-|-|
|There are no players available to revive right now!|If there are no dead teammates|
## Notes
- At the end of the dungeon run, all Revive Stones disappear.
## Bugs
- A Revive Stone can be transferred from a [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profile. However, it will show up as an "Unknown Item" and cannot be claimed.

|Sep 30, 2026|Revive Stones now work in solo dungeons|
|Sep 30, 2026|Revive Stones are now stackable.|

A Rose Dragon Pet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Farming]] pet purchased in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] from [[Ludleth]].
## Obtaining
A Rose Dragon Egg can be purchased from [[Ludleth]]. The Rose Dragon Egg costs 500,000,000 [[Coins]], 20,000 [[Copper]], 5 [[Condensed Helianthus]], 1 [[Glasscorn]], 1 [[Devourer]], 1 [[All-in Aloe]], 1 [[Phantomleaf]], and 1 [[Timestalk]].
## Usage
Both the Egg and the Pet have the type of [[Farming]] Pet. From level 1 to level 99, the Rose Dragon Egg has no stats nor abilities, and cannot hold [[Pet Items]].
Upon reaching level 100, it will hatch into a Rose Dragon Pet and gain its stats and perks.

## Trivia
- Rose Dragon Pet is the only Dragon pet that does not increase [[Magic Find]], however, it does increase [[Overbloom]], the farming equivalent for Magic Find.
- As of version 0.27, the Rose Dragon Pet's abilities grant up to 180 , 89.7 and 30 [[Farming Fortune]] respectively, up to a total of 299.7 [[Farming Fortune]], without the base 40 from its stats.
- As with other Dragon pets, the symbiosis ability doesn't count other instances of the same Dragon pet for additional Fortune.
## History

|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Changed Dragon's Gluttony to grant [[Overbloom]].|

# Royal Pigeon
Aliases: Pigeon
Categories: Legendary items, Items, UI Subpages, Tools, Mining
[Royal Pigeon]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Source: Commission Milestone 5 ([[Commissions]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ROYAL_PIGEON
Tooltip: Ability: Seek the King RIGHT CLICK Reach out to the King and open the Commissions menu.; Cooldown: 5s; * Co-op Soulbound * LEGENDARY
The Royal Pigeon is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
A Royal Pigeon can be claimed once the player reaches [[Commissions|Commission Milestone V]], which requires at least 500 [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]] or [[Glacite Tunnels]] [[Commissions]] to be completed.
## Usage
Every 5 seconds, the player can use the Royal Pigeon to access the [[Commissions|Commissions menu]] remotely from within the [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Tunnels]] or [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
A Royal Pigeon can be returned to [[Queen Mismyla]] to obtain her [[Abiphones|Abiphone contact]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|On Cooldown|This ability is on cooldown for #s.|
|Outside Mining Areas|The Royal Pigeon can't find the King from here.|
## Tips
- It is possible to right click the Royal Pigeon and then quickly swap to a mining tool with [[Compact]] before the menu opens to increase your [[Mining]] XP gain while claiming [[Commissions]].
## Trivia
- The Royal Pigeon is a reference to the fact that carrier pigeons have been used as messengers for thousands of years.
- Despite the Royal Pigeon's ability being unavailable outside of the [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Tunnels]] and [[Glacite Mineshafts]], calling [[Queen Mismyla]] on an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] will open the [[Commissions|Commissions menu]] regardless of the player's location.
- Trying to use the Royal Pigeon outside of the Dwarven Mines would previously display the message, "You may not use this Item outside the Dwarven Caves!".
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jan 30, 2026|Added Royal Pigeon to [[Museum]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Royal Pigeon.|
|Jan 20, 2021|Reduced [[Commissions]] required to unlock from 1,000 to 500.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Added Royal Pigeon to [[Rusty]]'s shop.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Royal Pigeons are now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Fixed error message in Royal Pigeon referring to Dwarven Mines as "Dwarven Caves".|
|Apr 9, 2024|0.20|Changed the error message from "You may not use this item outside the Dwarven Mines!" to "The Royal Pigeon can't find the King from here."|

The Runaan's Bow is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Bows|Bow]] unlocked at [[Bone|Bone IX (9)]]. It shoots 3 arrows at once.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Bone IX (9) ([[Bone]])|192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Bone]] 192x [[Enchanted String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Bone]] x64 ×3 + [[Enchanted String]] x64 ×3 → Runaan's Bow|
## Usage
Bow:
Runaan's Bow [[Damage]]: +160 [[Strength]]: +50
[Effects]
[Ability: Triple Shot; Shoots 3 arrows at a time! The 2 extra arrows deal 40% of the damage and home to targets.]
The Runaan's Bow fires 2 additional arrows at 12.5° from the main arrow. These arrows home towards targets within 10[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] blocks and deal 40% of the damage of the main arrow. [[Power]] and [[Piercing]] do not work on them.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Spider Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|20|20|
|✪|5|25|
|✪✪|10|35|
|✪✪✪|15|50|
|✪✪✪✪|20; 10,000 [[Coins]]|70; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|25; 25,000 [[Coins]]|95; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- Sometimes, the side arrows hit their target before the main arrow, causing the damage from the main arrow to be nullified due to invincibility frames.
- Runaan's Bow, and its counterpart the [[Hurricane Bow]], are references to League of Legends' Runaan's Hurricane, a bow which fires multiple arrows.
- After the Aiming enchantment was removed, no arrows homed. However, on June 11th 2021, the bow was changed so that the two side arrows home to any target and not just Dragons.
- This bow is often used as a good early game bow, as they are cheap to buy, and deal good initial damage.

The Sand Castle Barn Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Barn Skins|Barn Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Sand Castle Barn Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on July 6, 2024. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 6,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Sand Castle Barn Skin can be consumed on the [[The Garden|Garden]] and then used at [[The Garden|The Desk]] to change the look of the center plot.

# Sangria Dye
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Sangria Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_SANGRIA
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #00A86B!; X Rift-Transferable X LEGENDARY DYE
Sangria Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
A Sangria Dye can be obtained from killing any tier of the [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]. The drop chance depends on what tier of the slayer boss is killed. This chance is not affected by [[Magic Find]].
|Slayer Tier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Tier I|0.00100% (1 in 100,000)|
|Tier II|0.00125% (1 in 80,000)|
|Tier III|0.00167% (1 in 60,000)|
|Tier IV|0.00250% (1 in 40,000)|
|Tier V|0.01% (1 in 10,000)|
## Usage
Sangria Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #D40808. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

- Sangria Dyed Leather Armor
- Sangria Bucket of Dye Sangria [[Bucket of Dye]]

# Saul's Recommendation
Categories: Legendary items, Items
[Saul's Recommendation]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Adventurer Saul]]
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SAULS_RECOMMENDATION
Tooltip: Give this letter to Mort to gain access to enter The Catacombs on your own!; * Soulbound * LEGENDARY
Saul's Recommendatiom is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item. It is [[Soulbound]].
## Obtaining
The player is given a Saul's Recommendation after talking to [[Adventurer Saul]] once having beaten [[The Professor]] on [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Floor III]] of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. If the player drops or loses the item, they can talk to [[Adventurer Saul]] to get another Recommendation at no cost.
## Usage
The player can give [[Mort]] Saul's Recommendation to grant themselves the ability to queue solo dungeons.

Scarf's Grimoire can be salvaged for 240 (3 stacks plus 48) [[Wither Essence]].

Being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], Scarf's Grimoire can be transferred into the [[Rift Dimension]]. There, it grants +12s [[Rift Time]].

# Scorched Power Crystal

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SCORCHED_POWER_CRYSTAL
Tooltip: Powers Beacons for up to 48 hours at a time!; Adds +1% minion speed buff and +20%; profile stats to beacons fueled by this power crystal!; Has a single use and can be stacked.; LEGENDARY
The Scorched Power Crystal is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that is a [[Drop Chance|5Rare]] drop from the [[Inferno Demonlord]] at Tier III or higher. It can be used to power [[Beacon|Beacons]] for 48 hours.
## Obtaining
The Scorched Power Crystal can rarely drop from Tier III or higher [[Inferno Demonlord|Inferno Demonlords]] if the player has [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer LVL IV (4)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier III: 3.24% (1/30.83)
- Tier IV: 3.05% (1/32.81)
## Usage
The Scorched Power Crystal can be used to power Beacons. Beacons powered by Scorched Power Crystals will give 1% more [[Minions|Minion Speed]] and 20% more of the beacon's active [[Stats|stat]] as compared to usage with the [[Power Crystal]].
## Trivia
- The Scorched Power Crystal can be used even without [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer IV (4)]].
